    Resilience                                                           迎難向前

    unprecedented period of uncertainty.                                 專注於穩定業務基礎。


    Mainland China grew revenue by 8% in local currency terms,           中國內地業務的收入以當地貨幣計算增長 8%，
    leading the Group’s overall recovery, with both our core brands,     帶領本集團的整體復甦，維他奶及維他核心品牌
    VITASOY and VITA growing. We also opened our new Dongguan            產品銷售持續增長。儘管面對疫情帶來的種種挑
    factory on time despite the challenges posed by the pandemic.        戰，本集團東莞的新廠房亦如期開始運作。


    COVID-19 restrictions continued to impact Hong Kong in the           2019冠狀病毒病疫情實施的限制措施持續影響香
    important on-the-go and on-premise channels, greatly affecting       港的便利店及食肆等銷售渠道，亦大幅影響了維
    our Vitaland school tuckshop business. As a result, our revenue      他天地的學校小賣部業務。因此，香港業務的收
    from the Hong Kong Operation fell 12%.                               入下跌 12%。

    as easing of COVID-19 restrictions gradually helped our business     措施有所放寬後，業務於下半年度逐步復甦，收
    to recover in the second half of the year. The drop in revenue was   入以當地貨幣計算下跌 1%。收入下跌亦由於過
    also caused by a distortion from shoppers’ stock up which took       去財政年度最後一個月（即二零二零年三月），消
    place in the last month of the prior year (March 2020).              費者大批購入存貨所致。


    Singapore revenue was flat due to declines in beverage and export    新加坡業務的收入持平，原因是進口飲品及豆腐
    business offsetting our growth in domestic tofu sales.               出口業務均下跌，抵銷了當地豆腐銷售增長。

    to COVID-19 continuous lockdowns and extended restrictions.          施下，一次性飲用支裝產品業務的降幅。

Group Chief Executive Officer’s Report/Business Review
集團行政總裁報告╱業務回顧



In FY2020/2021, we continued to broaden our impact while             於二零二零╱二零二一財政年度，儘管全球飽受
navigating the unique challenges set by the COVID-19 pandemic.       2019冠狀病毒病的疫情肆虐，本集團仍能繼續應
Group revenue grew 4% driven by Mainland China delivering a          對疫情帶來的獨特挑戰，向前邁進。隨著中國內
solid 11% increase as its overall market and economic limitations    地整體市場及各項經濟限制措施開始放寬，中國
began to ease in the second half.                                    內地業務收入增長 11%，由此帶動本集團收入錄
                                                                     得 4% 增長。


Profits from operations increased 19% compared to FY2019/2020.       經營溢利較二零一九╱二零二零財政年度增長
As the Group decided to maintain headcount and employment            19%。儘管本集團受到疫情衝擊，導致我們在學
of our staff despite imploding revenues in Hong Kong due to the      校、便利店及食肆等銷售渠道的香港營運收入嚴
pandemic significantly affecting our school, on-the-go and on-       重下跌，但本集團仍決定堅守僱員人數及僱用，
premise channels, we became eligible for government support,         故此合資格申請政府補貼，主要為保就業計劃。

framework across the Group to consistently anticipate, assess            用一致的流程以預測、評估及減輕關鍵業務風
and mitigate key business risks, and a risk governance structure to      險，並採納風險管治架構，確保風險得到適當歸
ensure proper risk ownership and oversight. In view of the rapidly       屬及監督。鑑於營商環境瞬息萬變，本集團內部
changing business environment, Group Internal Audit and Risk             審計及風險管理部因而加強提升關鍵風險指標，
Management Department has stepped up efforts in improving key            識別外來新興風險，促進關鍵採購決策風險審
risk indicators, identifying external emerging risks, and facilitating   核。該等風險管理流程的詳情載於本年報內企業
risk reviews for key purchasing decisions. Details of these risk         管治報告的風險管理一節。
management processes are covered in the Risk Management

     Business Outlook                                                       展望
     The pandemic challenges will continue to impact the Group’s            疫情帶來的各種挑戰在短期內仍會影響本集團的
     operating environment in the short term, causing not only              營商環境，不單導致原材料及物流成本上升，亦
     increasing raw material and logistic costs, but also complexity and    令營運更添難度及不確定性。
     volatility.

     In this context, the Group’s sales outlook is cautiously optimistic.   在此情況下，本集團對銷售前景是審慎樂觀。


     Our operations proved resilient and are now operating within this      我們的業務維持穩健，並適應了在「新常態」中
     “new normal”. With the pandemic limitations slowly and gradually       營運。隨著疫情限制措施在我們各市場中逐漸緩
     easing across our markets, we expect to complete the recovery of       慢放寬，我們預計業務可在最受影響的銷售渠道
     our business in the sectors which have been most affected, like on-    中恢復，例如便利店及餐飲業務、所有市場的出
     the-go and on-premise channels, export across all markets and the      口，以及對香港業務作出重要貢獻的維他天地學
     material Vitaland school tuckshop business in Hong Kong.               校小賣部業務。


     We will continue to drive our growth vectors of improved               我們將繼續推動各增長模式，提升現有客戶的執
     execution in existing customers, and geographical expansion            行力，並在中國內地進行地域擴張。我們對已計
     primarily in Mainland China. Our innovation pipeline is strong,        劃的創新產品富有信心，並隨時準備逐步作出適
     relevant and ready to be progressively deployed. The global plant-     當部署。以植物為本的食品及飲品逐漸成為全球
     based food and beverages movement is becoming ever more                主流，預計將對本集團的前景增添增長動力。
     mainstream, and is expected to add additional impetus to the

Conclusion                                                            總結
Throughout our 80 years’ history, the Vitasoy Group has proved        縱觀我們過去 80 年的歷史，維他奶集團在面對
resilient in the face of diverse disruption and adversity. We will    各種逆境困難時依然保持業務穩健。我們將繼續
continue to focus on the safety of our teams and infrastructure,      專注保障團隊及基礎設施的安全，維持財務穩健
sustain growth upon firm financial discipline and thus advance our    審慎以維持增長，為我們的社區持續提供以植物
purpose to deliver sustainable plant-based tasty and nutritional      為本的美味及營養產品，繼續推進我們的增長目
products to our communities.                                          標。


I sincerely thank our Executive Chairman and our Board of             本人衷心感謝執行主席及董事會所給予的堅定支
Directors for their unwavering support, wise counsel and guidance     持、真知灼見和悉心指導。本人亦謹此感謝業務
in such unique times. I am grateful to all our business partners      夥伴一如既往的信任，以及本公司所有員工的熱
for their continued resolve, and to all of our associates for their   切忠誠與貢獻。
passionate dedication and contribution.

     Board of Directors                                                         董事會
     The general management of the Company’s business is vested                 董事會負責本公司業務之整體管理工作，並已成
     in the Board. The Board has established various committees to              立不同之委員會來履行由董事會委派且需具備特
     carry out specified functions assigned by the Board of Directors           定專業知識範疇之特定職能。董事會委任本公司
     which require specialised areas of expertise. The Board has                之執行董事及高層管理人員負責管理本公司日常
     delegated the day-to-day management power of the Company to                事務。然而，根據《公司條例》、上市規則、企業
     the Executive Directors and Senior Management of the Company.              管治守則或其他規管要求規定，部分特定事項不
     However, full delegation is not allowed for some specific matters          允許全權委託他人負責，並須由全體董事會成員
     under the Companies Ordinance, the Listing Rules, the CG Code              共同作出最終決定。董事會已採納如下明確之事
     or other regulatory requirements, and the final decisions on those         項表，列載須待董事會作出決定之事項：
     specific matters are required to be taken by the whole Board. A
     defined schedule of matters reserved for Board decisions has
     been adopted by the Board as follows:

                                      策略及方向

     The Board strives to achieve high standards of corporate                   董事會致力達致高標準的企業管治常規及履行本
     governance practices as well as the Company’s mission to creating          公司為股東創造價值之使命。董事會負責制訂本
     value for our shareholders. The Board is responsible for developing        公司之策略性方向，並持續監察本公司整體管理
     the strategic directions for the Company and continuous                    表現。制訂策略規劃已成為董事會實現使命和願
     monitoring of the performance of the general management of                 景以及維持長遠業務增長的重要職能，董事會因
     the Company. Strategic planning is one of the Board’s important            而非常重視並致力制訂及執行策略規劃。
     functions for aligning mission and vision and sustaining long term
     business growth and lot of focus and attention have been devoted
     to formulation and execution of the strategic planning.

     Strategic planning horizons are currently set for five years. The          策略規劃目前設定為五年。董事會已於二零二零
     Board has approved a Five Year Strategic Plan for the fiscal               年八月批准截至二零二五╱二零二六財政年度止
     years up to 2025/2026 in August 2020 and has always been                   之五年策略規劃，並一直因應宏觀經濟、競爭業
     taking a proactive role in reviewing and revising the Strategic            務環境及監管規條之變動，積極定期檢討及修訂
     Plan periodically in response to changes in the macro economic,            策略規劃。董事會亦積極制定本公司之可持續發
     competitive business environment and regulatory requirements.              展策略及常規，並優先考慮及分配資源以達致業
     The Board is also actively to define the Company’s sustainability          務之長久成功。

     Board Diversity                                                              董事會成員多元化
     The Board has adopted a Board Diversity Policy to set out the                董事會於二零一三年六月採納董事會成員多元化
     objectives and the factors to be considered for achieving the                政策，當中載列董事會成員多元化的目的以及達
     diversity of the Board in June 2013. The Board Diversity Policy              致多元化的考慮因素。董事會成員多元化政策已
     has been published on the Company’s website.                                 刊登於本公司網站。


     The Company recognises and embraces the benefits of having a                 本公司認同董事會成員多元化之裨益，並認為可
     diverse Board to enhance the quality of its performance. Board               增強董事會之表現質素。董事之委任及續聘過往
     appointment and renewal have been, and will continue to be,                  根據並將繼續按候選人之功績以及能否補替及加
     made based on merit as well as complementing and expanding                   強董事會整體之技能、知識及經驗為準則。薪酬

     annually and review such objectives, whenever applicable to                  目標。薪酬及提名委員會已制定截至二零二四╱

     Board Proceedings                                                           董事會程序
     The Board met five times in FY2020/2021 to discuss and approve              董事會於二零二零╱二零二一財政年度內召開五
     the Group’s long term strategic plan; to review and monitor the             次會議，討論及批准本集團之長期策略規劃；審
     financial and business performance of the Group and each of                 視及監察本集團及各營運單位之財務及業務表
     its operating units; to consider and approve annual budget, the             現；考慮及審批年度預算及財務報告；審閱及批
     financial reports; to review and approve sustainability report and          准可持續發展報告及與企業管治及可持續發展相
     various corporate governance and sustainability related policies            關之各種政策及常規；並監察及審視本集團風險
     and practices; and to monitor and review the effectiveness of the           管理表現之有效性。
     Group’s risk management performance.

     The Board and its Committees are supplied with full and timely              董事會及其委員會均適時獲得本公司所有相關資
     information in relation to the Company, which enables them to               料，讓其能履行職責。本公司已有既定程序讓董
     discharge their responsibilities. There are established procedures          事在適當的情況下，為履行其職責及責任而諮詢
     for Directors to seek independent professional advice for them to           獨立專業意見，費用由本公司支付。
     discharge their duties and responsibilities, where appropriate, at
     the Company’s expenses.

     Board Committees meetings and the Annual General Meeting                    會議及股東週年大會。

During the year, Dr. Roy Chi-ping CHUNG, an Independent Non-                         年內，本公司獨立非執行董事鍾志平博士獲委任
executive Director of the Company, has been appointed as the new                     為薪酬及提名委員會新成員，以進一步提高委員
member of the Remuneration and Nomination Committee in order                         會在制定本公司薪酬政策、釐定每位執行董事及
to further enhance the independence of the Committee in setting                      高層管理人員之薪酬待遇及訂立表現相關獎勵方
the Company’s remuneration policy, determining remuneration                          案之目標時持有之獨立性。在評估董事會成員之
package of each Executive Director and Senior Management and                         技能、特點和表現以及為董事會挑選出最佳董事
setting the targets for performance-related pay schemes. The                         候選人時，薪酬及提名委員會獨立性之增加亦顯
independence role of Remuneration and Nomination Committee                           重要。
has also increased in evaluating the skills, characteristics and
performance of board members and selecting the best candidates
for each seat on the Board.

      Major responsibilities                                               主要職責
      – to consider and approve the remuneration packages of               –   審批本集團執行董事及高層管理人員之薪酬
        Executive Directors and Senior Management of the Group,                福利條件，包括薪金、實物利益及花紅；花
        including salaries, benefits in kind and bonuses; bonus schemes        紅計劃及其他長期激勵計劃，包括購股權、
        and other long-term incentive schemes, including share option,         股份獎勵及其他計劃；

The Board has approved a Whistleblowing Policy in March 2012        於二零一二年三月，董事會已批准採納舉報政
which has been revised in November 2017. The main objective         策，並於二零一七年十一月作出修訂。政策之主
of the Policy is to provide employees and external parties an       要目標是為僱員及外間人士提供一個公開及統一
open and unified reporting channel and procedures to report         之舉報渠道及程序，舉報任何涉及本公司及其僱
any serious misconduct or malpractice involving the Company         員之嚴重失當或不良行為。審核委員會獲委派全
and its employees. The Audit Committee has been delegated           權負責監察及檢討舉報政策之成效。年內，概無
with the overall responsibility for monitoring and reviewing the    從僱員或外間人士接獲涉及嚴重欺詐或失職之事
effectiveness of the Whistleblowing Policy. During the year, no     件而對或將對本公司之財務狀況及整體營運造成
incident involving serious fraud or misconduct have been received   重大影響。
from employees nor external parties which had or would have a
material impact on the Company’s financial position and overall
operations.

a basic framework for developing the Company’s policies and         策及常規方面建立基本框架，確保能符合各股
practices to ensure alignment of interests and expectations from    東、公眾投資者及其他持份者的利益與期望。企
our shareholders, the investing public and other stakeholders.      業管治政策已刊登於本公司網站。

Risk Management Process                                                  風險管理程序
Our risk management process is embedded in our daily operations,         我們之風險管理程序已納入日常營運範圍、財務
financial activities, compliance, strategy development & business        活動、合規、策略發展與業務規劃、投資決定及
planning, investment decision and external factors. The processes        外界因素，程序如下：
are as follows:

•    Anticipate: Potential risks and threats that are related to         •   預測： 所有員工均參與識別不同業務流程
     different business processes and relevant to the Company’s              及與本公司目標相關之潛在風險及威脅。
     objectives will be anticipated and identified by all individual         為便於進行適當評估，我們將風險分為六
     staff. Risks are grouped into six clusters (including reputation,       個範疇（包括信譽、策略、市場、流動資
     strategy, market, liquidity, credit and operation) to facilitate        金、信貸及營運）。
     the assessment appropriately.

•    Rate: Each identified risk is evaluated on the basis of             •   評估： 按照董事會設定之風險承受能力，
     impact and likelihood consistent with the risk appetite set             評估每項經識別風險的影響及可能性。風
     by the Board. The risk impact and likelihood will be rated              險之影響及可能性將根據過往記錄、統計
     according to the historical records, statistical analyses               分析、相關經驗及管理層之專業判斷進行
     and relevant experiences, specialist and expert judgments               評級。集團風險管理促進評估流程，包括
     by management. Group Risk Management facilitates the                    協助企業風險管理執行委員會及審核委員
     evaluation process including the summary discussion in the              會之討論。
     Enterprise Risk Management Executive Committee and
     Audit Committee.

•    Respond: A delegated management is assigned to each risk            •   控制： 各項風險將配對專責管理人員，成
     as risk owner to fully respond on each risk. Risk management            為風險負責人，以有效管理風險。風險負
     strategies and corresponding control owners are identified              責人制定風險管理策略以及指派監控負責
     by risk owner to deploy appropriate control activities.                 人，實施適當監控活動。


•    Regulate: We take a close monitoring on the current status          •   監察： 我們密切監控風險現況。各風險現
     of risk, which is being monitored through key risk indicators           況的結果取決於關鍵風險指標的預設量度
     with defined thresholds, occurrence of related incidents,               級別基準、所發生之相關事故、內部監控
     control effectiveness and audit results. Any change in                  程序之成效以及審計結果。風險現況倘出
     current status of risk to a worsened level will trigger a react         現任何不良轉變，均會觸發反應程序，展
     process through creation of a mitigation plan.                          開減輕風險計劃。


•    React: When there is a change on the current status of              •   緩解： 當風險現況有所改變，相應負責人
     risk, the corresponding owner will coordinate mitigation                將協調減輕風險計劃之行動細節、負責人
     plan with action details, responsible person and timeline to            及時間表，以處理風險情況。
     address the status.

     Risk Management Activities                                            風險管理措施
     •    Improvement of Key Risk Indicators (“KRIs”)                      •   改進關鍵風險指標（「關鍵風險指標 」）

          risk status. During this year, the definition and threshold of       狀 況 的 主 要 工 具 之 一。 年 內， 我 們 為 生
          the KRIs for manufacturing, product quality, occupational            產、產品質素、職業健康及安全以及資訊
          health & safety, and information security have been                  安全之關鍵風險指標的定義及門檻進行了
          reviewed and revised where necessary. This resulted in a             審視，並於有需要時作出了修訂。此程序
          more timely and accurate detection of change in risk status          令我們能夠更及時、更準確地偵測風險狀
          and development of mitigation actions.                               況的變動，及制定減輕風險行動。


     •    Facilitated risk reviews for key purchases                       •   為關鍵採購進行風險審視

          key purchasing decisions. The objective of the review is to          中。審視目的是確保主要採購風險獲充分
          ensure that critical purchasing risks are adequately assessed        評估及考慮，藉此提升採購決策之質素。
          and considered and hence improve the quality of purchasing
          decisions.

     •    Enhanced emerging risk monitoring                                •   加強監察新興風險
          Vitasoy has stepped up the efforts in proactively scanning the       維他奶加大力度主動監測經營環境中的風
          risk environment to identify any emerging risks of potential         險， 以 識 別 任 何 潛 在 憂 慮 可 能 帶 來 之 風
          concerns, including pandemic, regulatory, sustainability,            險，如疫情、監管、可持續性、供應鏈及
          supply chain and other business risks. Additionally, Vitasoy         其他業務風險。此外，維他奶現正進一步
          is further exploring the use of data analytics solutions to          研究使用數據分析解決方案以提高風險監
          improve the efficiency and effectiveness of risk monitoring.         察之效益和成效。


     •    Enterprise Integrity Risk Management                             •   企業誠信風險管理
          To support the on-going development of integrity culture,            為支持誠信文化持續發展，維他奶制定企
          Vitasoy launched the Enterprise Integrity Risk Management            業誠信風險管理政策，訂明管理誠信風險
          policy to specify supervisory accountability in managing             時之監督責任，旨在加強第一線員工發現
          integrity risk, aiming to strengthen the first-line awareness        異常情況並採取補救措施之意識。
          in the detection of and remediation actions on abnormalities.

     Dividend Policy                                                       股息政策
     The Board has adopted a Dividend Policy to provide guidance to        董事會已採納股息政策，就派發盈餘資金予股東
     the Board and the management of the Company for distribution          向董事會及管理層提供指引。
     of surplus funds to its shareholders.

     In determining the level of dividend payment, the following           就決定所派付的股息水平，須計及下列準則及考
     criteria and consideration must be taken:                             慮因素：


     –    Sharing of success with shareholders                             –   與股東共享成果
     –    Liquidity and capital requirement                                –   流動資金及資本要求
     –    Historical trend of dividend payout                              –   過往股息派付之趨勢
     –    Benchmarking with competitors within the industry                –   與業內競爭對手之標準比較
     –    Fulfilment of financial covenants                                –   符合財務契約
     –    Taxation impact                                                  –   稅務影響
     –    Restrictions                                                     –   限制條件
     –    Other factors that may deem relevant                             –   任何其他視為相關之因素


     The Company intends to declare dividends to shareholders semi-        本公司計劃每半年向股東宣派股息，每年宣派之
     annually in an aggregate amount per year of approximately 60%         股息總額預計為扣除一次性事件影響後之股東預
     of the anticipated consolidated net annual profit attributable        期應佔年度綜合溢利約 60%，並可能除中期或末
     to shareholders net of one-off event impact and may declare           期股息外不時宣派特別股息。董事會將不時酌情
     special dividends from time to time in addition to interim or         決定是否派發股息以及所派發股息之水平。
     final dividends. The determination to pay dividends and level of
     dividend to be distributed will be subject to the discretion of the
     Board from time to time.

Opinion                                                                   意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of                  本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審計列載於第
Vitasoy International Holdings Limited (“the Company”) and its            106 至 239 頁的維他奶國際集團有限公司（以下
subsidiaries (“the Group”) set out on pages 106 to 239, which             簡稱「貴公司」）及其附屬公司（以下統稱「貴集
comprise the consolidated statement of financial position as at           團」）的綜合財務報表，此財務報表包括於二零
31st March 2021, the consolidated statement of profit or loss, the        二一年三月三十一日的綜合財務狀況表與截至該
consolidated statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive          日止年度的綜合損益表、綜合損益及其他全面收
income, the consolidated statement of changes in equity and               益表、綜合權益變動表和綜合現金流量表，以及
the consolidated cash flow statement for the year then ended              綜合財務報表附註，包括主要會計政策概要。
and notes to the consolidated financial statements, including a
summary of significant accounting policies.

In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true         我們認為，該等綜合財務報表已根據香港會計師
and fair view of the consolidated financial position of the Group as      公會頒布的《香港財務報告準則》真實而中肯地
at 31st March 2021 and of its consolidated financial performance          反映了   貴集團於二零二一年三月三十一日的綜
and its consolidated cash flows for the year then ended in                合財務狀況及    貴集團截至該日止年度的綜合財
accordance with Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards                   務表現及綜合現金流量，並已遵照香港《公司條
(“HKFRSs”) issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public          例》妥為擬備。
Accountants (“HKICPA”) and have been properly prepared in

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether        我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存在

issue an auditor’s report that includes our opinion. This report is    是按照香港《公司條例》第 405 條的規定，僅向
made solely to you, as a body, in accordance with section 405 of       整體成員報告。除此以外，我們的報告不可用作
the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance, and for no other purpose.           其他用途。我們概不就本報告的內容，對任何其
We do not assume responsibility towards or accept liability to any     他人士負責或承擔法律責任。
other person for the contents of this report.

guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs             港審計準則》進行的審計，在某一重大錯誤陳述
will always detect a material misstatement when it exists.             存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引
Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are considered         起，如果合理預期它們單獨或匯總起來可能影響
material if, individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably   綜合財務報表使用者依賴財務報表所作出的經濟
be expected to influence the economic decisions of users taken on      決定，則有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。
the basis of these consolidated financial statements.

      (e) Joint venture                                                       (e) 合營公司
      A joint venture is an arrangement whereby the Group or Company          合營公司為本集團或本公司及其他人士按合約同
      and other parties contractually agree to share control of the           意下分配控制權及淨資產擁有權之安排。
      arrangement, and have rights to the net assets of the arrangement.

      An investment in a joint venture is accounted for in the                除合營公司的投資被分類為持作出售或納入被分
      consolidated financial statements under the equity method,              類為持作出售的出售組別外，於合營公司的投資
      unless it is classified as held for sale (or included in a disposal     是按權益法記入綜合財務報表，並且先以成本入
      group that is classified as held for sale). Under the equity method,    賬，另調整本集團於收購日應佔該投資的可辨別
      the investment is initially recorded at cost, adjusted for any excess   淨資產之公允值所超出成本之任何金額（如有）。
      of the Group’s share of the acquisition-date fair values of the         投資成本包括購買價格、直接歸屬於收購投資之
      investee’s identifiable net assets over the cost of the investment      其他成本及對構成本集團股權投資一部分之合營
      (if any). The cost of the investment includes purchase price, other     公司之任何直接投資。往後，需調整在收購後本
      costs directly attributable to the acquisition of the investment,       集團應佔該投資淨資產之變動及在附註 1(f) 及 (k)
      and any direct investment into the joint venture that forms part        (ii) 所載有關該投資的減值損失。任何超出收購日

      the investee’s net assets and any impairment loss relating to the       認，而本集團應佔該投資收購後和已除稅之其他
      investment (see notes 1(f) and (k)(ii)). Any acquisition-date excess    全面收益項目則在綜合損益及其他全面收益表內
      over cost, the Group’s share of the post-acquisition, post-tax          確認。

      on behalf of the investee. For this purpose, the Group’s interest is    集團所佔權益是以按照權益法計算投資的賬面金

(f) Goodwill                                                            (f)    商譽
Goodwill represents the excess of:                                      商譽指：


(i)    the aggregate of the fair value of the consideration             (i)    已轉讓代價之公允值、於被收購方任何非
       transferred, the amount of any non-controlling interest in              控股權益金額與本集團先前持有被收購方
       the acquiree and the fair value of the Group’s previously               股本權益公允值之總和；超出
       held equity interest in the acquiree; over

(ii)   the net fair value of the acquiree’s identifiable assets and     (ii)   於收購日期計量之被收購方可識別資產及
       liabilities measured as at the acquisition date.                        負債之公允值淨額之部份。


When (ii) is greater than (i), then this excess is recognised           倘第 (ii) 項高於第 (i) 項，該差額即時於損益確認
immediately in profit or loss as a gain on a bargain purchase.          為議價收購之收益。


Goodwill is stated at cost less accumulated impairment losses.          商譽按成本值減去累計減值虧損後列賬。企業合
Goodwill arising on a business combination is allocated to each         併產生之商譽分配至預計將會受惠於合併之協同
cash-generating unit, or groups of cash generating units, that is       作用之各現金產生單位或現金產生單位之組別，
expected to benefit from the synergies of the combination and is        並於每年進行減值測試（見附註 1(k)(ii)）。
tested annually for impairment (see note 1(k)(ii)).

attributable amount of purchased goodwill is included in the            計入購入商譽之任何應佔金額。
calculation of the profit or loss on disposal.

Write-off policy                                                          撇銷政策
The gross carrying amount of a financial asset is written off (either     倘金融資產並無實際收回前景，則其賬面總值
partially or in full) to the extent that there is no realistic prospect   （部分或全部）將會被撇銷。該情況通常出現在本
of recovery. This is generally the case when the Group determines         集團認為債務人並無資產或收入來源可產生足夠
that the debtor does not have assets or sources of income that            現金流量以償還將予撇銷之金額。
could generate sufficient cash flows to repay the amounts subject
to the write-off.

(ii)   Impairment of other assets                                         (ii)   其他資產之減值
Internal and external sources of information are reviewed at              本集團於每個報告期末檢討內部及外間資料來
the end of each reporting period to identify indications that the         源，以確定下列資產有否出現減值跡象，或過
following assets may be impaired or, except in the case of goodwill,      往已確認之減值虧損不再存在或已減少（商譽除
an impairment loss previously recognised no longer exists or may          外）：
have decreased:

–      property, plant and equipment, including right-of-use assets       –      物業、廠房及設備，包括使用權資產及投
       and investment properties;                                                資物業；


–      intangible assets;                                                 –      無形資產；


–      goodwill;                                                          –      商譽；


–      interest in joint venture; and                                     –      合營公司之權益；及


–      investment in subsidiaries in the Company’s statement of           –      於本公司財務狀況表的附屬公司之投資。
       financial position.

If any such indication exists, the asset’s recoverable amount is          倘有任何減值跡象，則會估計該項資產之可收回
estimated. In addition, for goodwill, the recoverable amount              金額。此外，就商譽而言，不論是否有任何減值
is estimated annually whether or not there is any indication of           跡象存在，亦於每年估計其可收回金額。
impairment.

      –      Calculation of recoverable amount                                    –      計算可收回金額


             The recoverable amount of an asset is the greater of its fair               資產之可收回金額為其公允值減去出售成
             value less costs of disposal and value in use. In assessing                 本及使用價值兩者中之較高者。於評估使
             value in use, the estimated future cash flows are discounted                用價值時，會使用除稅前折現率將估計未
             to their present value using a pre-tax discount rate that                   來現金流量折現至其現值。該折現率反映
             reflects current market assessments of time value of money                  市場當時所評估之貨幣時間價值和該資產
             and the risks specific to the asset. Where an asset does not                之獨有風險。倘個別資產所產生之現金流
             generate cash inflows largely independent of those from                     入基本上不能獨立於其他資產所產生之現
             other assets, the recoverable amount is determined for                      金流入，則就獨立產生現金流入之最小資
             the smallest group of assets that generates cash inflows                    產組合（即現金產生單位）釐定可收回金
             independently (i.e. a cash-generating unit).                                額。


      –      Recognition of impairment losses                                     –      確認減值虧損


             An impairment loss is recognised in profit or loss if the                   資產或其所屬之現金產生單位之賬面值高
             carrying amount of an asset, or the cash-generating unit                    於其可收回金額時，則會於損益確認減值
             to which it belongs, exceeds its recoverable amount.                        虧損。就現金產生單位確認之減值虧損會
             Impairment losses recognised in respect of cash-generating                  首先分配予削減已分配至該現金產生單位
             units are allocated first to reduce the carrying amount of any              （或一組單位）之任何商譽之賬面值，然後
             goodwill allocated to the cash-generating unit (or group of                 按比例削減該單位（或一組單位）內其他資
             units) and then, to reduce the carrying amount of the other                 產之賬面值，惟資產賬面值不可下調至低
             assets in the unit (or group of units) on a pro rata basis, except          於其個別公允值減去出售成本（如能計量）
             that the carrying value of an asset will not be reduced below               或使用價值（如能釐定）。
             its individual fair value less costs of disposal (if measurable),
             or value in use (if determinable).

      –      Reversals of impairment losses                                       –      撥回減值虧損


             In respect of assets other than goodwill, an impairment                     就商譽以外之資產而言，倘用以釐定可收
             loss is reversed if there has been a favourable change in the               回金額之估計發生有利變動，則會將減值
             estimates used to determine the recoverable amount. An                      虧損撥回。商譽之減值虧損不可撥回。
             impairment loss in respect of goodwill is not reversed.

             A reversal of an impairment loss is limited to the asset’s                  所撥回之減值虧損僅限於倘若並無於過往
             carrying amount that would have been determined had no                      年度確認減值虧損而可釐定之資產賬面
             impairment loss been recognised in prior years. Reversals of                值。所撥回之減值虧損在確認撥回之年度
             impairment losses are credited to profit or loss in the year in             內計入損益。
             which the reversals are recognised.

estimated costs necessary to make the sale.

profit is further adjusted for items not specifically attributed         於個別分部之項目（如融資成本、所佔合營公司
to individual segments, such as finance costs, share of losses of        虧損、合營公司權益之減值虧損及未分配之總公
joint venture, impairment loss on interest in joint venture and          司及企業費用）作出進一步調整。所得稅並無列
unallocated head office and corporate expenses. Income tax is not        入報告分部。分部間銷售乃按成本加邊際利潤定
allocated to reporting segments. Inter-segment sales are priced at       價。
cost plus a profit margin.

      (Continued)
The fair value measurement is categorised as a Level 3 valuation     公允值計量根據《香港財務報告準則》第 13 號
under the three-level fair value hierarchy as defined in HKFRS       「公允值計量 」所界定之三級公允值架構分類為第
13, Fair value measurement. The level into which a fair value        三級估值。將某公允值計量分類之等級乃經參考
measurement is classified is determined with reference to            如下估值方法所用數據之可觀察性及重要性後釐
observability and significance of the inputs used in the valuation   定：
technique as follows:

–     Level 1 valuations: Fair value measured using only Level 1     –     第一級估值：僅使用第一級輸入數據（即於
      inputs i.e. unadjusted quoted prices in active markets for           計量日同類資產或負債於活躍市場之未經
      identical assets or liabilities at the measurement date;             調整報價）計量之公允值；


–     Level 2 valuations: Fair value measured using Level 2 inputs   –     第二級估值：使用第二級輸入數據（即未能
      i.e. observable inputs which fail to meet Level 1, and not           達到第一級之可觀察輸入數據）且並未使用
      using significant unobservable inputs. Unobservable inputs           重大不可觀察輸入數據計量之公允值。不
      are inputs for which market data are not available; and              可觀察輸入數據為無市場數據之輸入數據；
                                                                           及


–     Level 3 valuations: Fair value measured using significant      –     第三級估值：使用重大不可觀察輸入數據
      unobservable inputs.                                                 計量之公允值。


The fair value of investment properties is determined by using       投資物業之公允值乃參考可比較物業之市價使用
direct comparison approach with reference to the market price        直接比較法釐定，並就參考交易之樓宇質素及時
of comparable properties and adjusted for building quality and       間作出調整。公允值計量之重大不可觀察輸入數
timing of the reference transaction. The significant unobservable    據乃個別物業調整率，介乎 0.95 至 1.05（二零二
input in the fair value measurement is the property-specific         零年：0.95 至 1.05）。公允值計量與個別物業調
adjusting rate, which ranged from 0.95 to 1.05 (2020: 0.95 to        整率之間成正向關係。
1.05). The fair value measurement is positively correlated to the
property-specific adjusting rate.

Impairment testing for cash-generating unit                          包含商譽之現金產生單位之減值測試
containing goodwill

Vitasoy International Singapore Pte. Ltd. (“VIS”).                   Ltd.（「VIS」）之全部股本而產生。


The recoverable amount of the VIS operation is determined based      VIS 業 務 之 可 收 回 金 額 乃 根 據 使 用 價 值 計 算 釐
on a value-in-use calculation. That calculation uses cash flow       定。該計算使用按管理層批准之五年期（二零二
projections based on financial budgets approved by management        零年：五年期）財政預算得出之現金流量預測。
covering a five-year period (2020: five-year period). Cash           五年期（二零二零年：五年期）以後之現金流量
flows beyond the five-year period (2020: five-year period) are       則使用 3%（二零二零年：3%）之年增長率推算。
extrapolated using an annual growth rate of 3% (2020: 3%). The       該增長率不超過 VIS 業務所經營業務之長期平均
growth rate does not exceed the long-term average growth rate        增長率。
for the business in which the VIS operation operates.

Key assumptions used for the value-in-use calculation:               計算使用價值時採用之主要假設如下：


                                                                                   2021                   2020
                                                                              二零二一年                 二零二零年


 – Gross margin                                                                   34.3%                  34.0%
 – 毛利率
 – Average annual sales growth rate for the five-year period                      10.6%                   9.4%
 – 五年期之平均年銷售增長率
 – Pre-tax discount rate                                                           8.8%                   8.8%
 – 除稅前折現率


Management determined the budgeted gross margin and sales            管理層根據過往表現及其對市場發展之預測，確
growth rate based on past performance and its expectation            定預算毛利率及銷售增長率。所使用之折現率則
for market development. The discount rate used is pre-tax and        為反映相關分部特定風險之除稅前折現率。
reflects specific risks relating to the relevant segment.

Management believes that any reasonably possible change in the       管理層認為，釐定可收回金額所依據之主要假設
key assumptions on which the recoverable amount is based would       出現任何合理可能變動，均不會導致賬面值超過
not cause the carrying amount to exceed its recoverable amount.      其可收回金額。

      25 Capital, reserves and dividends (Continued)                         25 資本、儲備及股息（續）
      (d) Nature and purpose of reserves                                     (d) 儲備之性質及用途
      (i)    Capital reserve                                                 (i)    資本儲備
      As part of the restructuring of the Group in February 1994, the        作為本集團於一九九四年二月進行之重組之一部
      Company disposed of a property to a former subsidiary and              份，本公司向一間前附屬公司出售一項物業，代
      consideration was received in the form of cash and another             價以現金及另一項物業支付，故自該交易產生總
      property. A total gain, representing the difference between            收益，即所出售物業之歷史賬面值與已收代價公
      the historical carrying value of the property disposed of and          允值之差額。
      the fair value of the consideration received, resulted from such
      transaction.

      The gain arising from this transaction was divided into realised       該項交易之收益按已收現金及物業（「物業」）公
      and unrealised portions in the ratio of the amount of cash and the     允值之比例分為已變現及未變現部份。未變現收
      fair value of the property received (“the property”). The unrealised   益已計入資本儲備，並於計算物業折舊時變現。
      gain was taken to capital reserve and is realised on depreciation      於年內，1,218,000 元（二零二零年：1,218,000
      of the property. During the year, $1,218,000 (2020: $1,218,000)        元）自資本儲備轉撥至保留溢利。
      was transferred from capital reserve to retained profits.

      (ii)   Surplus reserve                                                 (ii)   盈餘儲備
      The surplus reserve has been set up by Shenzhen Vitasoy, Vitasoy       盈餘儲備由深圳維他奶、佛山維他奶、武漢維他
      Foshan, Vitasoy Wuhan, Vitasoy Shanghai and Vitasoja (Macau)           奶、上海維他奶及維他奶（澳門）有限公司分別
      Limitada, in accordance with regulations in the PRC and Macau          根據中國及澳門之法規設立。
      respectively.

      (iii) Other reserve                                                    (iii) 其他儲備
      The other reserve arose from the equity transactions with non-         其他儲備產生自深圳維他奶及東莞維他奶分別於
      controlling interests of Shenzhen Vitasoy in 2011 and Vitasoy          二零一一年及二零一九年與非控股權益之權益交
      Dongguan in 2019.                                                      易。


      (iv) Hedging reserve                                                   (iv) 對沖儲備
      The hedging reserve comprises the effective portion of the             對沖儲備包括用作有待其後根據附註 1(t) 所載就
      cumulative net change in the fair value of hedging instruments         現金流量對沖採納之會計政策確認對沖現金流量
      used in cash flow hedges pending subsequent recognition of             之現金流量對沖之對沖工具的公允值累計淨變動
      the hedged cash flow in accordance with the accounting policy          之實際部份。
      adopted for cash flow hedges in note 1(t).

      (v)    Exchange reserve                                                (v)    匯兌儲備

(f) Capital management                                                 (f)   資本管理
The Group’s primary objectives when managing capital are to            本集團在管理資本時之首要目標為保障本集團能
safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern,          持續經營的能力，透過將產品及服務的價格訂於
so that it can continue to provide returns for shareholders and        與風險相稱的水平及按合理成本籌措融資，使其
benefits for other stakeholders, by pricing products and services      能繼續為股東提供回報及為其他利益相關人士提
commensurately with the level of risk and by securing access to        供利益。
finance at a reasonable cost.

The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages its capital       本集團積極及定期對資本架構進行檢討及管理，
structure to maintain a balance between the higher shareholder         以在較高股東回報可能附帶較高借貸水平與雄厚
returns that might be possible with higher levels of borrowings        資本狀況帶來之優勢及保障間取得平衡，並因應
and the advantages and security afforded by a sound capital            經濟環境之變化對資本架構作出調整。
position, and makes adjustments to the capital structure in light of
changes in economic conditions.

Consistent with industry practices, the Group monitors its capital     本著業內慣例，本集團以負債對經調整資本比率
structure on the basis of a debt-to-adjusted capital ratio. For this   作為監控其資本架構之基準。就此目的，本集團
purpose, the Group defines debt as the total of bills payable, bank    將負債界定為應付票據、銀行貸款及租賃負債之
loans and lease liabilities, and adjusted capital as all components    總額，經調整資本為權益之所有組成部份減非累
of equity less unaccrued proposed dividends.                           計擬派股息。

(a) Credit risk                                                          (a) 信貸風險
Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will default on its   信貸風險指交易方違反其合約責任而導致本集團
contractual obligations resulting in a financial loss to the Group.      財務虧損之風險。


The Group’s credit risk is primarily attributable to trade and other     本集團之信貸風險主要來自應收賬款及其他應收
receivables. Management has a credit policy in place and exposure        款。管理層備有信貸政策，而所面臨之有關信貸
to credit risk is monitored on an ongoing basis.                         風險持續受到監控。


The Group’s cash and bank deposits are placed with financial             本集團之現金及銀行存款乃存放於有良好信貸評
institutions with sound credit ratings, and the management               級之金融機構，管理層認為本集團面臨之信貸風
consider the Group’s exposure to credit risk is low.                     險為低。


The Group does not provide any guarantees which would expose             本集團並無提供任何將令本集團面臨信貸風險之
the Group to credit risk.                                                擔保。

(c) Interest rate risk                                                           (c) 利率風險

of a financial instrument will fluctuate because of changes in                   應市場利率變動而波動之風險。
market interest rates.

bearing borrowings. The Group monitors the level of its fixed rate               察其定息及浮息借貸水平。本集團由管理層監控
and variable rate borrowings. The Group’s interest rate profile as               之利率資料載於下文第 (i) 項。
monitored by management is set out in (i) below.
